        function clock() {
            var now = new Date();
            var outStr = now.getHours() + ':' + now.getMinutes() + ':' + now.getSeconds();
            if (document.getElementById('clockDiv')) {
                document.getElementById('clockDiv').innerHTML = outStr;
            }
            setTimeout('clock()', 1000);
        }
        clock();
    </script>

            var obj = JSON.parse(jQuery("#form\\:records").val());

            function updateChart(DOMId, url) {
                var chartObj = getChartFromId(DOMId);
                chartObj.setDataURL(url);
            }

            jQuery().ready(function() {
                obj.forEach(function(e){
                    var chart = new FusionCharts(e.chartName , "ChartId"+e.order, e.width, e.height, "0", "1");
                    chart.setDataURL("/nims/faces/report/online.chart?channelType="+e.channelType+e.chartType+e.mode+e.allBank+"000000001"+"&channelId="+ e.id);
                    chart.render("chartdiv"+ e.order);
                });

                jQuery(this).everyTime(30000, 'controlled', function() {
                    obj.forEach(function(e){
                        updateChart('ChartId'+ e.order, '/nims/faces/report/online.chart?channelType='+e.channelType+e.chartType+e.mode+e.allBank+"000000001"+"&channelId="+ e.id);
                    });
                });

    document.onkeydown = function(e) {
        if (e == null) { // ie
            keycode = event.keyCode;
        } else { // mozilla
            keycode = e.which;
        }
        if (keycode == 27) { // escape, close box
            closeAllLOVS();
            alertDialog.hide();
        }
    };

    function fireAlerts() {

        if (jQuery("#form\\:hasAlert").val() == "true") {

            makeSound();

            try {
                alertDialog.show();
            } catch(err) {
            }

        } else {

            document.getElementById("beep").innerHTML = "";
            if (jQuery("div[aria-labelledby='ui-dialog-title-form:alertT1']").css('display') != 'none') {
                try {
                    alertDialog.hide();
                } catch(err) {
                }
            }


        }


    }

     function menuSelectEvent(value) {
         document.getElementById("form:selectedMenu").value = value;
         menuSelectAction();
     }


     function makeSound() {
        document.getElementById("beep").innerHTML = '<embed src="/resources//songs/beep-1.mp3" type="application/x-mplayer2" height="0" width="0" playcount="3"></embed>';
    }
